STRATEGY\012============================================================\012\012DO NOT attempt to create the entire world as one giant uncontrolled mutation.\012\012Work in controlled waves.\012\012Required macro sequence:\012\012WAVE 0 — PREFLIGHT / RECOVERY\012WAVE 1 — WORLD ARCHITECTURE\012WAVE 2 — ASSET LIBRARY AUDIT\012WAVE 3 — TERRAIN MACRO PASS\012WAVE 4 — FOREST KIT / FOREST DISTRIBUTION\012WAVE 5 — GEOLOGY / ROCK DISTRIBUTION\012WAVE 6 — LANDMARKS / NAVIGATION / CLEARINGS\012WAVE 7 — AMBIENT CREATURE FRAMEWORK\012WAVE 8 — CREATURE POPULATION\012WAVE 9 — RESOURCE / GAMEPLAY WORLD INTEGRATION\012WAVE 10 — LIGHTING / ATMOSPHERE / AUDIO\012WAVE 11 — PERFORMANCE / STREAMING / DENSITY\012WAVE 12 — FULL PLAYTEST / DEBUG\012WAVE 13 — INDEPENDENT TECHNICAL + GAMEPLAY + VISUAL REVIEW\012WAVE 14 — FINAL FIX LOOP\012WAVE 15 — OVERNIGHT DIRECTOR CHECKPOINT\012\012You may adapt details based on actual project state, but do not skip major gates\012without explicit evidence that they are irrelevant/already complete.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 0 — PREFLIGHT\012============================================================\012\012Freshly discover the connected Roblox Studio.\012\012Never reuse a stale studio_id from an old session.\012\012Verify:\012\012- correct Bunker Game place;\012- Edit mode;\012- Roblox Studio MCP reachable;\012- BloxForge MCP reachable;\012- canonical project directory;\012- Rojo binary;\012- backup boundary exists;\012- current filesystem/Rojo source state;\012- current world state;\012- current approved Asset Library;\012- current generated asset registry;\012- current gameplay-critical world anchors.\012\012If Studio is unexpectedly in Play before filesystem work:\012\012STOP PLAY.\012\012Verify:\012\012Current Studio Mode=Edit\012Available DataModels=Edit\012\012before continuing.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 1 — WORLD ARCHITECTURE\012============================================================\012\012Invoke REAL Qwen through bunkergame-master-reasoner.\012\012Have Qwen reason about:\012\012- macro terrain hierarchy;\012- bunker relationship to surrounding wilderness;\012- traversal;\012- player orientation;\012- forest density zones;\012- sightline rhythm;\012- open/closed spaces;\012- hill/ridge placement;\012- valleys/depressions;\012- spawn safety;\012- landmarks;\012- navigation;\012- gameplay resource areas;\012- creature habitat regions;\012- performance/density constraints.\012\012Then Muse converts that reasoning into a concrete art-direction plan.\012\012The world should feel large because of composition, occlusion, elevation,\012landmarks and controlled traversal — not simply because every coordinate is\012filled with objects.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 2 — ASSET LIBRARY AUDIT\012============================================================\012\012Audit approved reusable assets.\012\012Identify:\012\012- tree families;\012- dead-tree variants;\012- fallen-tree variants;\012- rock/boulder families;\012- bunker exterior props;\012- vegetation;\012- debris;\012- environmental storytelling props;\012- creature assets if any.\012\012Prefer reuse.\012\012If the existing tree kit is already visually strong, USE IT.\012\012Do not regenerate five more trees just because generation is available.\012\012Generate only genuinely missing diversity.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 3 — TERRAIN MACRO PASS\012============================================================\012\012Build an intentionally authored terrain macrostructure.\012\012Target characteristics:\012\012- large playable wilderness;\012- meaningful elevation variation;\012- hills;\012- ridges;\012- shallow valleys;\012- depressions;\012- flatter gameplay areas;\012- clear bunker approach zones;\012- forest floor variation;\012- natural transitions;\012- no giant random noise spikes;\012- no impossible cliffs unless intentionally used as boundary/landmark;\012- no terrain clipping through critical bunker geometry;\012- no spawn burial;\012- no blocked entrances.\012\012Terrain should create:\012\012- concealment;\012- reveal;\012- tension;\012- navigational rhythm;\012- visual hierarchy.\012\012After terrain pass:\012\012- BloxForge technical QA;\012- Muse visual inspection;\012- filesystem/Rojo checkpoint;\012- Studio verification.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 4 — FOREST\012============================================================\012\012Use approved tree families first.\012\012The forest must not look like evenly spaced procedural poles.\012\012Use ecological-looking clusters:\012\012- dense pockets;\012- medium-density transition zones;\012- sparse edges;\012- young-tree groups;\012- dead trees;\012- occasional fallen trees;\012- clearings;\012- gaps around paths;\012- silhouette variety.\012\012Use variation in:\012\012- rotation;\012- approved scale ranges;\012- family variant;\012- spacing;\012- clustering;\012- edge density.\012\012Do not use absurd random scaling.\012\012Preserve player navigation.\012\012Do not block:\012\012- spawn;\012- bunker doors;\012- farming areas;\012- interaction anchors;\012- major traversal corridors.\012\012Where existing trees have foliage:\012\012- foliage should generally not physically block the player unless intentional;\012- trunks may collide;\012- collision should remain performant.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 5 — GEOLOGY\012============================================================\012\012Use approved rock/boulder families.\012\012Create believable geological grouping.\012\012Avoid:\012\012- one rock every exactly N studs;\012- uniform rotations;\012- random floating rocks;\012- half-buried geometry without intention;\012- rocks blocking doors;\012- excessive collision complexity.\012\012Use:\012\012- individual boulders;\012- clusters;\012- partial burial;\012- slope-edge formations;\012- landmark rocks;\012- small/medium/large hierarchy.\012\012BloxForge must verify grounding, scale, collision and intersections.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 6 — LANDMARKS / NAVIGATION\012============================================================\012\012Create readable environmental navigation.\012\012The player should be able to form a mental map.\012\012Use features such as:\012\012- distinctive hill/ridge;\012- rock formation;\012- major dead tree;\012- clearing;\012- bunker sightline;\012- trail;\012- ruined/environmental storytelling element;\012- lighting/silhouette landmark.\012\012Do not cover every location with props.\012\012Negative space is intentional.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 7 — AMBIENT CREATURE FRAMEWORK\012============================================================\012\012Do NOT create an entirely separate AI architecture for every animal.\012\012Build one reusable lightweight ambient creature framework.\012\012It should support configurable species/variants.\012\012Desired behavior as appropriate:\012\012- idle;\012- wander;\012- pause;\012- turn;\012- roam within habitat bounds;\012- avoid obvious obstacles;\012- maintain reasonable distance from critical player spawn;\012- optionally flee when players approach;\012- despawn/sleep at excessive distance if needed;\012- multiplayer-safe behavior;\012- server-authoritative where required;\012- low network/CPU cost.\012\012Ambient creatures are WORLD ATMOSPHERE first.\012\012They are not required to become full combat NPCs.\012\012Do not confuse them with zombies.\012\012Use simple state machines/configuration where appropriate.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 8 — CREATURE POPULATION\012============================================================\012\012Populate only after the framework is validated.\012\012Examples may include suitable wilderness background life such as:\012\012- deer-like distant wildlife;\012- rabbit/small animal equivalents;\012- birds or other ambient silhouettes if technically appropriate.\012\012Choose species according to the game's established environment/art direction.\012\012Do not turn the map into a zoo.\012\012Use sparse, believable habitat-based populations.\012\012If appropriate creature visual assets do not exist:\012\012- Qwen determines the reference strategy;\012- Roblox Assistant/native generation creates a small reusable kit;\012- BloxForge QA;\012- Muse visual review;\012- register approved variants;\012- persist through Rojo.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 9 — GAMEPLAY WORLD INTEGRATION\012============================================================\012\012Make the world support gameplay.\012\012Audit/integrate as appropriate:\012\012- harvestable trees;\012- resource nodes;\012- wood;\012- resource respawn;\012- inventory;\012- farming access;\012- bunker access;\012- zombie navigation;\012- nighttime traversal;\012- economy/resource zones.\012\012Do not make every decorative tree harvestable unless architecture intends it.\012\012Clearly separate:\012\012- decorative environment assets;\012- gameplay resource nodes;\012\012while maintaining visual consistency.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 10 — LIGHTING / ATMOSPHERE / AUDIO\012============================================================\012\012Preserve and improve the creepy survival tone.\012\012Review:\012\012- Lighting;\012- Atmosphere;\012- fog;\012- ColorCorrection;\012- Bloom;\012- SunRays;\012- DepthOfField where appropriate;\012- environmental audio;\012- wind/forest ambience;\012- distant wildlife ambience;\012- bunker/exterior transition.\012\012Avoid destroying gameplay readability.\012\012Night should be tense, not simply pitch-black and unusable.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 11 — PERFORMANCE\012============================================================\012\012Large map != unlimited instances.\012\012Audit:\012\012- total instance count;\012- MeshParts;\012- opportunities for reuse.\012\012Prefer asset families and reuse over thousands of unique generated meshes.\012\012If StreamingEnabled is considered, do not casually enable it without checking\012gameplay/system assumptions.\012\012Performance fixes must preserve the intended visual composition.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 12 — Prefer to trigger Rojo sync: check if Rojo plugin sync can be triggered via Studio command. If not, manually create Workspace.GameWorld and Workspace.Runtime by loading the JSON via Luau (parse and clone). As fallback, use bloxforge asset insertion? Find the cleanest method that results in Workspace containing GameWorld and Runtime matching filesystem.

3. After fix, verify via search_game_tree that Workspace.GameWorld with children Foundation, Bunker, Exterior, Farm, Resources, EnemySpawns, Objectives, Presentation exists, and Workspace.Runtime exists (empty Folder).

LINE=62
PATH=part.state.input.code
KEYWORDS=tree,boulder,REALISTIC_TREE_KIT,BoulderShowcase,Destroy(),:Destroy,Workspace
-- Clean stray Workspace content not in filesystem source
local ws = game.Workspace
for _, name in ipairs({"REALISTIC_TREE_KIT", "BoulderShowcase"}) do
    local obj = ws:FindFirstChild(name)
    if obj then
        print("Destroying " .. name .. " (" .. obj.ClassName .. ")")
        obj:Destroy()
    else
        print("Not found: " .. name)
    end
end
-- Find and remove the standalone boulder model (name starts with realistic weathered)
for _, child in ipairs(ws:GetChildren()) do
    if child.Name:find("realistic weathered") then
        print("Destroying stray model: " .. child.Name)
        child:Destroy()
    end
end
print("Cleanup done. Workspace children:")
for _, c in ipairs(ws:GetChildren()) do
    print(" - " .. c.Name .. " (" .. c.ClassName .. ")")
end
return "cleanup_complete"

historical information but should not silently\012become the new source of truth.\012\012============================================================\012WORLD DEVELOPMENT STRATEGY\012============================================================\012\012DO NOT attempt to create the entire world as one giant uncontrolled mutation.\012\012Work in controlled waves.\012\012Required macro sequence:\012\012WAVE 0 — PREFLIGHT / RECOVERY\012WAVE 1 — WORLD ARCHITECTURE\012WAVE 2 — ASSET LIBRARY AUDIT\012WAVE 3 — TERRAIN MACRO PASS\012WAVE 4 — FOREST KIT / FOREST DISTRIBUTION\012WAVE 5 — GEOLOGY / ROCK DISTRIBUTION\012WAVE 6 — LANDMARKS / NAVIGATION / CLEARINGS\012WAVE 7 — AMBIENT CREATURE FRAMEWORK\012WAVE 8 — CREATURE POPULATION\012WAVE 9 — RESOURCE / GAMEPLAY WORLD INTEGRATION\012WAVE 10 — LIGHTING / ATMOSPHERE / AUDIO\012WAVE 11 — PERFORMANCE / STREAMING / DENSITY\012WAVE 12 — FULL PLAYTEST / DEBUG\012WAVE 13 — INDEPENDENT TECHNICAL + GAMEPLAY + VISUAL REVIEW\012WAVE 14 — FINAL FIX LOOP\012WAVE 15 — OVERNIGHT DIRECTOR CHECKPOINT\012\012You may adapt details based on actual project state, but do not skip major gates\012without explicit evidence that they are irrelevant/already complete.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 0 — session.\012\012Verify:\012\012- correct Bunker Game place;\012- Edit mode;\012- Roblox Studio MCP reachable;\012- BloxForge MCP reachable;\012- canonical project directory;\012- Rojo binary;\012- backup boundary exists;\012- current filesystem/Rojo source state;\012- current world state;\012- current approved Asset Library;\012- current generated asset registry;\012- current gameplay-critical world anchors.\012\012If Studio is unexpectedly in Play before filesystem work:\012\012STOP PLAY.\012\012Verify:\012\012Current Studio Mode=Edit\012Available DataModels=Edit\012\012before continuing.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 1 — WORLD ARCHITECTURE\012============================================================\012\012Invoke REAL Qwen through bunkergame-master-reasoner.\012\012Have Qwen reason about:\012\012- macro terrain hierarchy;\012- bunker relationship to surrounding wilderness;\012- traversal;\012- player orientation;\012- forest density zones;\012- sightline rhythm;\012- open/closed spaces;\012- hill/ridge placement;\012- valleys/depressions;\012- spawn safety;\012- landmarks;\012- navigation;\012- gameplay resource areas;\012- creature habitat regions;\012- performance/density constraints.\012\012Then Muse converts that reasoning into a concrete art-direction plan.\012\012The world should feel large because of composition, occlusion, elevation,\012landmarks and controlled traversal — not simply because every coordinate is\012filled with objects.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 2 — ASSET LIBRARY AUDIT\012============================================================\012\012Audit approved environmental storytelling props;\012- creature assets if any.\012\012Prefer reuse.\012\012If the existing tree kit is already visually strong, USE IT.\012\012Do not regenerate five more trees just because generation is available.\012\012Generate only genuinely missing diversity.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 3 — TERRAIN MACRO PASS\012============================================================\012\012Build an intentionally authored terrain macrostructure.\012\012Target characteristics:\012\012- large playable wilderness;\012- meaningful elevation variation;\012- hills;\012- ridges;\012- shallow valleys;\012- depressions;\012- flatter gameplay areas;\012- clear bunker approach zones;\012- forest floor variation;\012- natural transitions;\012- no giant random noise spikes;\012- no impossible cliffs unless intentionally used as boundary/landmark;\012- no terrain clipping through critical bunker geometry;\012- no spawn burial;\012- no blocked entrances.\012\012Terrain should create:\012\012- concealment;\012- reveal;\012- tension;\012- navigational rhythm;\012- visual hierarchy.\012\012After terrain pass:\012\012- BloxForge technical QA;\012- Muse visual inspection;\012- filesystem/Rojo checkpoint;\012- Studio verification.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 4 — FOREST\012============================================================\012\012Use approved tree families first.\012\012The forest must not look like evenly spaced procedural poles.\012\012Use ecological-looking clusters:\012\012- dense pockets;\012- medium-density transition zones;\012- sparse edges;\012- young-tree groups;\012- dead trees;\012- occasional fallen trees;\012- clearings;\012- gaps around paths;\012- silhouette variety.\012\012Use variation in:\012\012- rotation;\012- approved scale ranges;\012- family variant;\012- spacing;\012- clustering;\012- edge density.\012\012Do not use absurd random scaling.\012\012Preserve player navigation.\012\012Do not block:\012\012- spawn;\012- bunker doors;\012- farming areas;\012- interaction anchors;\012- major traversal corridors.\012\012Where existing trees have foliage:\012\012- foliage should generally not physically block the player unless intentional;\012- trunks may collide;\012- collision should remain performant.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 5 — GEOLOGY\012============================================================\012\012Use approved rock/boulder families.\012\012Create believable geological grouping.\012\012Avoid:\012\012- one rock every exactly N studs;\012- uniform rotations;\012- random floating rocks;\012- half-buried geometry without intention;\012- rocks blocking doors;\012- excessive collision complexity.\012\012Use:\012\012- individual boulders;\012- clusters;\012- partial burial;\012- slope-edge formations;\012- landmark rocks;\012- small/medium/large hierarchy.\012\012BloxForge must verify grounding, scale, collision and intersections.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 6 — LANDMARKS / NAVIGATION\012============================================================\012\012Create readable environmental navigation.\012\012The player should be able to form a mental map.\012\012Use features such as:\012\012- distinctive hill/ridge;\012- rock formation;\012- major dead tree;\012- clearing;\012- bunker sightline;\012- trail;\012- ruined/environmental storytelling element;\012- lighting/silhouette landmark.\012\012Do not cover every location with props.\012\012Negative space is intentional.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 7 — AMBIENT CREATURE FRAMEWORK\012============================================================\012\012Do NOT create an entirely separate AI architecture for every animal.\012\012Build one reusable lightweight ambient creature framework.\012\012It should support configurable species/variants.\012\012Desired behavior as appropriate:\012\012- idle;\012- wander;\012- pause;\012- turn;\012- roam within habitat bounds;\012- avoid obvious obstacles;\012- maintain reasonable distance from critical player spawn;\012- optionally flee when players approach;\012- despawn/sleep at excessive distance if needed;\012- multiplayer-safe behavior;\012- server-authoritative where required;\012- low network/CPU cost.\012\012Ambient creatures are WORLD ATMOSPHERE first.\012\012They are not required to become full combat NPCs.\012\012Do not confuse them with zombies.\012\012Use simple state machines/configuration where appropriate.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 8 — CREATURE POPULATION\012============================================================\012\012Populate only after the framework is validated.\012\012Examples may include suitable wilderness background life such as:\012\012- deer-like distant wildlife;\012- rabbit/small animal equivalents;\012- birds or other ambient silhouettes if technically appropriate.\012\012Choose species according to the game's established environment/art direction.\012\012Do not turn the map into a zoo.\012\012Use sparse, believable habitat-based populations.\012\012If appropriate creature visual assets do not exist:\012\012- Qwen determines the reference strategy;\012- Roblox Assistant/native generation creates a small reusable kit;\012- BloxForge QA;\012- Muse visual review;\012- register approved 11 — PERFORMANCE\012============================================================\012\012Large map != unlimited instances.\012\012Audit:\012\012- total instance count;\012- MeshParts;\012- checking\012gameplay/system assumptions.\012\012Performance fixes must preserve the intended visual composition.\012\012============================================================\012WAVE 12 — roaming;\012- creature performance;\012- zombie/world coexistence;\012- day/night;\012- obvious console errors.\012\012============================================================\012DEBUG POLICY\012============================================================\012\012On recoverable errors:\012\012DO NOT immediately stop.\012\012Diagnose and fix.\012\012Potential known failure modes include:\012\0121. MIMO IS SUBAGENT\012   -> invoke bunker-builder through Task from Muse.\012   -> do not launch it as primary and silently fall back to another model.\012\0126. /tmp PERMISSION FAILURE\012   -> NEVER use /tmp.\012   -> retry with project-local evidence/build path.\012\0127. BLOXFORGE DIRECT MCP HAS NO REQUEST ID\012   -> record NONE_DIRECT_MCP.\012   -> do not invent bridge request IDs.\012\0128. ROJO PATH\012   -> use $HOME/.aftman/bin/rojo.\012\01210. ROJO VALIDATION OUTPUT\012   -> write inside project only.\012\01211. PLAY LEFT RUNNING\012   -> stop immediately and verify Edit before further source work.\012\01212. SCRIPT ERROR\012   -> inspect console/stack;\012   -> locate canonical filesystem source;\012   -> fix source;\012   -> validate;\012   -> sync;\012   -> rerun focused QA.\012\01213. CREATURE AI EXPENSIVE\012   -> reduce update frequency;\012   -> use state scheduling;\012   -> distance/sleep logic;\012   -> avoid per-frame server loops for every animal.\012\01214. NAVIGATION BLOCKED\012   -> fix placement/terrain/geometry, not by disabling all collision globally.\012\01215. ROJO SOURCE/LIVE DRIFT\012   -> determine intended canonical source.\012   -> never silently overwrite unknown important work.\012\01218. MCP DISCONNECT\012   -> retry/recover;\012   -> freshly inspect connection;\012   -> do not fabricate successful tool calls.\012\012============================================================\012STALL POLICY\012============================================================\012\012Status-only chatter is NOT progress.\012\012Meaningful progress means something like:\012\012- file/source change;\012- completed child task;\012- completed asset generation;\012- completed QA;\012- completed Rojo validation;\012- completed world wave;\012- completed Play QA;\012- resolved defect;\012- new evidence artifact.\012\012If no meaningful progress occurs for approximately 20 minutes:\012\0121. diagnose why;\0122. inspect current session/tool state;\0123. attempt recovery;\0124. continue.\012\012Do not repeatedly emit status messages while doing nothing.\012\012============================================================\012RECOVERY LIMIT\012============================================================\012\012For the same blocking failure, directories;\0126. continue to the next wave.\012\012Do not wait for human approval between ordinary overnight waves unless the\012decision genuinely cannot be made from existing art direction/technical\012constraints.\012\012============================================================\012MUSE VISUAL coordinates;\012- script output;\012- MeshId;\012- technical QA text.\012\012Capture/inspect actual player-facing views where available.\012\012Review:\012\012- macro silhouette;\012- terrain readability;\012- forest composition;\012- repetition;\012- grounding;\012- landmarks;\012- bunker/world relationship;\012- visual clutter;\012- scale;\012- creepy survival tone;\012- environmental LINE=74
PATH=part.state.output
KEYWORDS=terrain,forest,creature,edit,rojo,asset,bloxforge,muse,qwen,mimo
#!/bin/bash

set -u

ROOT="/Users/Ori/Documents/Projects/BunkerGame"
BASE="http://127.0.0.1:4098"

RUNROOT="$ROOT/.opencode/overnight_world"
PROMPTFILE="$RUNROOT/world_directive.txt"
LOG="$RUNROOT/watchdog.log"
STATEFILE="$RUNROOT/state.txt"
PROGRESS="$RUNROOT/progress.txt"
PIDFILE="$RUNROOT/watchdog.pid"
OCLOG="$RUNROOT/opencode4098.log"

RUNS="$RUNROOT/runs"
EVIDENCE="$RUNROOT/evidence"
CHECKPOINTS="$RUNROOT/checkpoints"
LOCK="$RUNROOT/watchdog.lock"

ROJO="$HOME/.aftman/bin/rojo"

MAX_RECOVERY=5
STALL_SECONDS=1200
CHECK_INTERVAL=60

mkdir -p "$RUNROOT" "$RUNS" "$EVIDENCE" "$CHECKPOINTS"

cd "$ROOT" || exit 1

ts() {
    date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
}

epoch() {
    date '+%s'
}

log() {
    echo "[$(ts)] $*" >> "$LOG"
}

set_state() {
    echo "$*" > "$STATEFILE"
    log "STATE=$*"
}

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$LOCK"
}

trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# SINGLE WATCHDOG INSTANCE
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! mkdir "$LOCK" 2>/dev/null; then
    echo "OVERNIGHT_WORLD_WATCHDOG_ALREADY_RUNNING=YES"
    exit 0
fi

echo $$ > "$PIDFILE"

log "============================================================"
log "OVERNIGHT WORLD WATCHDOG START"
log "PID=$$"
log "ROOT=$ROOT"
log "============================================================"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# BASIC HEALTH
# ------------------------------------------------------------

health_4098() {
    curl -fsS --max-time 4 \
        "$BASE/global/health" \
        >/dev/null 2>&1
}

wait_for_4098() {
    local i

    for i in $(seq 1 40); do
        if health_4098; then
            return 0
        fi
        sleep 2
    done

    return 1
}

recover_4098() {
    if health_4098; then
        return 0
    fi

    log "4098 unhealthy; attempting recovery."

    local pid
    pid="$(pgrep -f 'opencode serve --hostname 127.0.0.1 --port 4098' | head -1 || true)"

    if [ -n "$pid" ]; then
        log "Stopping stale 4098 PID=$pid"
        kill "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        sleep 4

        if kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
            log "4098 PID still alive; force terminating."
            kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
            sleep 2
        fi
    fi

    if lsof -nP -iTCP:4098 -sTCP:LISTEN >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
        log "Port 4098 still occupied."
        return 1
    fi

    log "Starting fresh OpenCode 4098."

    nohup opencode serve \
        --hostname 127.0.0.1 \
        --port 4098 \
        >>"$OCLOG" 2>&1 &

    if wait_for_4098; then
        log "4098_RECOVERY=PASS"
        return 0
    fi

    log "4098_RECOVERY=FAIL"
    tail -100 "$OCLOG" >> "$LOG" 2>&1 || true
    return 1
}

mcp_health() {
    local raw="$EVIDENCE/mcp_latest_raw.txt"
    local clean="$EVIDENCE/mcp_latest.txt"

    opencode mcp list >"$raw" 2>&1 || true

    # OpenCode MCP output is terminal-formatted. Strip ANSI/control
    # sequences before parsing so a healthy MCP list cannot become a
    # false negative merely because of presentation formatting.
    perl -pe 's/\e\[[0-9;?]*[ -\/]*[@-~]//g' "$raw" \
        | tr -d '\r' >"$clean"

    local has_roblox=0
    local has_bloxforge=0

    if grep -Fq 'roblox-studio' "$clean" &&
       grep -F 'roblox-studio' "$clean" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        # "connected" may be rendered on the line before the command,
        # so validate the complete server block/output rather than
        # assuming a single-line representation.
        if grep -Fq 'connected' "$clean"; then
            has_roblox=1
        fi
    fi

    if grep -Fq 'bloxforge' "$clean" &&
       grep -Fq 'connected' "$clean"; then
        has_bloxforge=1
    fi

    if [ "$has_roblox" -eq 1 ] &&
       [ "$has_bloxforge" -eq 1 ]; then
        log "MCP_HEALTH=PASS roblox-studio=connected bloxforge=connected"
        return 0
    fi

    log "MCP_HEALTH=FAIL roblox=$has_roblox bloxforge=$has_bloxforge"
    cat "$clean" >>"$LOG"
    return 1
}

rojo_health() {
    if [ ! -x "$ROJO" ]; then
        log "ROJO_MISSING=$ROJO"
        return 1
    fi

    "$ROJO" --version >>"$LOG" 2>&1 || return 1
    return 0
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# PROJECT-LOCAL VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

validate_project() {
    local stamp
    stamp="$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')"

    local sm="$CHECKPOINTS/sourcemap_$stamp.json"
    local build="$CHECKPOINTS/world_$stamp.rbxlx"

    log "Running Rojo sourcemap checkpoint."

    if ! "$ROJO" sourcemap default.project.json \
        --output "$sm" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
        log "ROJO_SOURCEMAP_CHECKPOINT=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    if [ ! -s "$sm" ]; then
        log "ROJO_SOURCEMAP_EMPTY=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    log "Running Rojo build checkpoint."

    if ! "$ROJO" build default.project.json \
        --output "$build" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
        log "ROJO_BUILD_CHECKPOINT=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    if [ ! -s "$build" ]; then
        log "ROJO_BUILD_EMPTY=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    log "ROJO_CHECKPOINT=PASS sourcemap=$sm build=$build"
    return 0
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# RESULT VALIDATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

is_true_final_pass() {
    local f="$1"

    grep -q 'WORLD_MACRO_LAYOUT=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'TERRAIN_PASS=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'FOREST_PASS=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'GEOLOGY_PASS=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'LANDMARK_NAVIGATION_PASS=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'AMBIENT_CREATURE_FRAMEWORK=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'AMBIENT_CREATURES=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'GAMEPLAY_WORLD_INTEGRATION=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'LIGHTING_ATMOSPHERE_AUDIO=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'PERFORMANCE_QA=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'PLAYTEST_QA=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'TECHNICAL_QA=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'GAMEPLAY_UX_QA=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'VISUAL_QA=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'ROJO_PERSISTENCE=YES' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'ROJO_VALIDATION=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'STUDIO_VERIFICATION=YES' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'STUDIO_MODE=Edit' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'OVERNIGHT_WORLD_BUILD=PASS' "$f" &&
    grep -q 'DIRECTOR_CHECKPOINT=READY' "$f"
}

extract_final() {
    local f="$1"

    grep -E \
'MUSE_INVOKED=|MUSE_SESSION_ID=|QWEN_INVOKED=|QWEN_SESSION_IDS=|QWEN_REFERENCE_SOURCES=|MIMO_INVOKED=|MIMO_SESSION_IDS=|ROBLOX_ASSISTANT_INVOKED=|ROBLOX_GENERATION_IDS=|BLOXFORGE_INVOKED=|BLOXFORGE_TOOLS=|BLOXFORGE_REQUEST_IDS=|ASSET_LIBRARY_REGISTERED=|ASSET_FAMILIES=|ASSET_VARIANTS=|WORLD_MACRO_LAYOUT=|TERRAIN_PASS=|FOREST_PASS=|GEOLOGY_PASS=|LANDMARK_NAVIGATION_PASS=|AMBIENT_CREATURE_FRAMEWORK=|AMBIENT_CREATURES=|GAMEPLAY_WORLD_INTEGRATION=|LIGHTING_ATMOSPHERE_AUDIO=|PERFORMANCE_QA=|PLAYTEST_QA=|TECHNICAL_QA=|GAMEPLAY_UX_QA=|VISUAL_QA=|ROJO_PERSISTENCE=|ROJO_VALIDATION=|STUDIO_VERIFICATION=|STUDIO_MODE=|OVERNIGHT_WORLD_BUILD=|DIRECTOR_CHECKPOINT=' \
        "$f" | tail -200 > "$EVIDENCE/final_result.txt" || true
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# PREFLIGHT RECOVERY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

set_state "PREFLIGHT"

RECOVERY=0

while ! recover_4098; do
    RECOVERY=$((RECOVERY + 1))

    if [ "$RECOVERY" -ge "$MAX_RECOVERY" ]; then
        set_state "BLOCKED_4098"
        log "error debug maxed out"
        exit 10
    fi

    sleep 10
done

RECOVERY=0

while ! mcp_health; do
    RECOVERY=$((RECOVERY + 1))
    log "MCP recovery attempt $RECOVERY/$MAX_RECOVERY"

    if [ "$RECOVERY" -ge "$MAX_RECOVERY" ]; then
        set_state "BLOCKED_MCP"
        log "error debug maxed out"
        exit 11
    fi

    sleep 15
done

    log "MCP_HEALTH=FAIL roblox=$has_roblox bloxforge=$has_bloxforge"
    cat "$clean" >>"$LOG"
    return 1
}

    if [ ! -x "$ROJO" ]; then

        return 1
    fi

    "$ROJO" --version >>"$LOG" 2>&1 || return 1
    return 0

        if kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
            log "4098 PID still alive; force terminating."
            kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
            sleep 2

mcp_health() {
    local raw="$EVIDENCE/mcp_latest_raw.txt"
    local clean="$EVIDENCE/mcp_latest.txt"

    opencode mcp list >"$raw" 2>&1 || true

    # OpenCode MCP output is terminal-formatted. Strip ANSI/control
    # sequences before parsing so a healthy MCP list cannot become a
    # false negative merely because of presentation formatting.
    perl -pe 's/\e\[[0-9;?]*[ -\/]*[@-~]//g' "$raw" \
        | tr -d '\r' >"$clean"

    local has_roblox=0
    local has_bloxforge=0

    if grep -Fq 'roblox-studio' "$clean" &&
       grep -F 'roblox-studio' "$clean" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        # "connected" may be rendered on the line before the command,
        # so validate the complete server block/output rather than
        # assuming a single-line representation.
        if grep -Fq 'connected' "$clean"; then
            has_roblox=1
        fi
    fi

    if grep -Fq 'bloxforge' "$clean" &&
       grep -Fq 'connected' "$clean"; then
        has_bloxforge=1
    fi

    if [ "$has_roblox" -eq 1 ] &&
       [ "$has_bloxforge" -eq 1 ]; then
        log "MCP_HEALTH=PASS roblox-studio=connected bloxforge=connected"
        return 0
    fi

    log "MCP_HEALTH=FAIL roblox=$has_roblox bloxforge=$has_bloxforge"
    cat "$clean" >>"$LOG"
    return 1
}

rojo_health() {
    if [ ! -x "$ROJO" ]; then
        log "ROJO_MISSING=$ROJO"
        return 1
    fi

    "$ROJO" --version >>"$LOG" 2>&1 || return 1
    return 0
}

        log "ROJO_SOURCEMAP_CHECKPOINT=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    if [ ! -s "$sm" ]; then
        log "ROJO_SOURCEMAP_EMPTY=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    log "Running Rojo build checkpoint."

    if ! "$ROJO" build default.project.json \
        --output "$build" >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
        log "ROJO_BUILD_CHECKPOINT=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    if [ ! -s "$build" ]; then
        log "ROJO_BUILD_EMPTY=FAIL"
        return 1
    fi

    log "ROJO_CHECKPOINT=PASS sourcemap=$sm build=$build"
    return 0
}

set_state "PREFLIGHT"

RECOVERY=0

while ! recover_4098; do
    RECOVERY=$((RECOVERY + 1))

    if [ "$RECOVERY" -ge "$MAX_RECOVERY" ]; then
        set_state "BLOCKED_4098"
        log "error debug maxed out"
        exit 10
    fi

    sleep 10
done

RECOVERY=0

while ! mcp_health; do
    RECOVERY=$((RECOVERY + 1))
    log "MCP recovery attempt $RECOVERY/$MAX_RECOVERY"

    if [ "$RECOVERY" -ge "$MAX_RECOVERY" ]; then
        set_state "BLOCKED_MCP"
        log "error debug maxed out"
        exit 11
    fi

    sleep 15
done

if ! rojo_health; then
    set_state "BLOCKED_ROJO"
    log "error debug maxed out"
    exit 12
fi

# Baseline validation is useful but not allowed to destroy the run if the
# repository already contains a known unrelated source issue.
if validate_project; then
    log "BASELINE_ROJO_VALIDATION=PASS"
else
    log "BASELINE_ROJO_VALIDATION=WARNING"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# MAIN AUTONOMOUS ATTEMPTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ATTEMPT=1

while [ "$ATTEMPT" -le "$MAX_RECOVERY" ]; do

    set_state "RUNNING_ATTEMPT_$ATTEMPT"

    STAMP="$(date '+%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')"
    OUT="$RUNS/world_attempt_${ATTEMPT}_${STAMP}.jsonl"

    log "Starting Muse world execution attempt=$ATTEMPT output=$OUT"

    START_SIZE=0
    LAST_SIZE=0
    LAST_PROGRESS="$(epoch)"

    # Run Muse in background so watchdog can observe it.
    opencode run \
        --attach "$BASE" \
        --dir "$ROOT" \
        --agent bunkergame-art-director \
        --model opencode/muse-spark-1.2-contributor-free \
        --format json \
        "$(cat "$PROMPTFILE")" \
        >"$OUT" 2>&1 &

    RUNPID=$!

    log "Muse run PID=$RUNPID"

    echo "$RUNPID" > "$RUNROOT/current_run.pid"
    echo "$OUT" > "$RUNROOT/current_run_file.txt"

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # WATCH CURRENT MUSE RUN
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    while kill -0 "$RUNPID" 2>/dev/null; do

        sleep "$CHECK_INTERVAL"

        # 4098 recovery.
        if ! health_4098; then
            log "4098 became unhealthy during active run."

            # Do not instantly kill Muse; first try server recovery.
            if ! recover_4098; then
                log "4098 recovery failed during active run."
            fi
        fi

        # Observe output size as one signal of progress.
        if [ -f "$OUT" ]; then
            SIZE="$(wc -c < "$OUT" | tr -d ' ')"
        else
            SIZE=0
        fi

        if [ "$SIZE" -gt "$LAST_SIZE" ]; then
            LAST_SIZE="$SIZE"
            LAST_PROGRESS="$(epoch)"
            echo "$(ts) attempt=$ATTEMPT bytes=$SIZE" > "$PROGRESS"
        fi

        NOW="$(epoch)"
        IDLE=$((NOW - LAST_PROGRESS))

        # Check for concrete evidence events too.
        if tail -120 "$OUT" 2>/dev/null | grep -Eq \
            '"type":"tool_use"|state="completed"|ROJO_|PASS|generation|BloxForge|Play|Studio Mode'; then
            LAST_PROGRESS="$NOW"
        fi

        if [ "$IDLE" -ge "$STALL_SECONDS" ]; then
            log "STALL_DETECTED attempt=$ATTEMPT idle_seconds=$IDLE"

            tail -200 "$OUT" \
                >"$EVIDENCE/stall_attempt_${ATTEMPT}_${STAMP}.txt" 2>/dev/null || true

            # End only the stuck opencode run, not Studio/BloxForge.
            kill "$RUNPID" 2>/dev/null || true
            sleep 5

            if kill -0 "$RUNPID" 2>/dev/null; then
                kill -9 "$RUNPID" 2>/dev/null || true
            fi

            break

        sleep 4

        if kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; then
            log "4098 PID still alive; force terminating."
            kill -9 "$pid" 2>/dev/null || true
            sleep 2
        fi
    fi

    if lsof -nP -iTCP:4098 -sTCP:LISTEN >>"$LOG" 2>&1; then
        log "Port 4098 still occupied."
        return 1

        return 0
